## Highlights
This release features approximately 660 commits from 251 contributors (86 new contributors).
\*\*Breaking Changes:\*\*
- \*\*Async scheduling is now enabled by default\*\* \- Users who experience issues can disable with `--no-async-scheduling`.
- Excludes some not-yet-supported configurations: pipeline parallel, CPU backend, non-MTP/Eagle spec decoding.
- \*\*PyTorch 2.9.1\*\* is now required and the default wheel is compiled against cu129.
- Deprecated quantization schemes have been removed ( [#31688](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31688), [#31285](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31285)).
- When using speculative decoding, unsupported sampling parameters will fail rather than being silently ignored ( [#31982](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31982)).
\*\*Key Improvements:\*\*
- \*\*Async scheduling enabled by default\*\* ( [#27614](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27614)): Overlaps engine core scheduling with GPU execution, improving throughput without user configuration. Now also works with speculative decoding ( [#31998](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31998)) and structured outputs ( [#29821](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29821)).
- \*\*gRPC server entrypoint\*\* ( [#30190](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30190)): Alternative to REST API with binary protocol, HTTP/2 multiplexing.
- \*\*`--max-model-len auto`\*\* ( [#29431](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29431)): Automatically fits context length to available GPU memory, eliminating OOM startup failures.
- \*\*Model inspection view\*\* ( [#29450](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29450)): View the modules, attention backends, and quantization of your model in vLLM by specifying `VLLM\_LOG\_MODEL\_INSPECTION=1` or by simply printing the `LLM` object.
- \*\*Model Runner V2 enhancements\*\*: UVA block tables ( [#31965](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31965)), M-RoPE ( [#32143](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32143)), `logit\_bias`/`allowed\_token\_ids`/`min\_tokens` support ( [#32163](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32163)).
- Please note that Model Runner V2 is still experimental and disabled by default.
### Model Support
\*\*New Model Architectures:\*\*
- Grok-2 with tiktoken tokenizer ( [#31847](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31847))
- LFM2-VL vision-language model ( [#31758](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31758))
- MiMo-V2-Flash ( [#30836](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30836))
- openPangu MoE ( [#28775](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28775))
- IQuestCoder ( [#31575](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31575))
- Nemotron Parse 1.1 ( [#30864](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30864))
- GLM-ASR audio ( [#31436](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31436))
- Isaac vision model v0.1/v0.2 ( [#28367](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28367), [#31550](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31550))
- Kanana-1.5-v-3b-instruct ( [#29384](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29384))
- K-EXAONE-236B-A23B MoE ( [#31621](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31621))
\*\*LoRA Support Expansion:\*\*
- Multimodal tower/connector LoRA ( [#26674](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26674)): LLaVA ( [#31513](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31513)), BLIP2 ( [#31620](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31620)), PaliGemma ( [#31656](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31656)), Pixtral ( [#31724](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31724)), DotsOCR ( [#31825](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31825)), GLM4-V ( [#31652](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31652))
- DeepSeek-OCR ( [#31569](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31569)), Qwen3-Next ( [#31719](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31719)), NemotronH ( [#31539](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31539)), PLaMo 2/3 ( [#31322](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31322))
- Vision LoRA mm\\_processor\\_cache support ( [#31927](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31927))
- MoE expert base\\_layer loading ( [#31104](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31104))
\*\*Model Enhancements:\*\*
- Qwen3-VL as reranker ( [#31890](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31890))
- DeepSeek v3.2 chat prefix completion ( [#31147](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31147))
- GLM-4.5/GLM-4.7 `enable\_thinking: false` ( [#31788](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31788))
- Ernie4.5-VL video timestamps ( [#31274](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31274))
- Score template expansion ( [#31335](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31335))
- LLaMa4 vision encoder compilation ( [#30709](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30709))
- NemotronH quantized attention ( [#31898](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31898))
### Engine Core
- \*\*Async scheduling default\*\* with spec decode ( [#27614](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27614), [#31998](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31998)) and structured outputs ( [#29821](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29821))
- \*\*Hybrid allocator + KV connector\*\* ( [#30166](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30166)) with multiple KV cache groups ( [#31707](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31707))
- Triton attention: encoder-only/cross attention ( [#31406](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31406)), cross-layer blocks ( [#30687](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30687))
- Mamba2 prefix cache optimization ( [#28047](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28047))
- Batch invariant LoRA ( [#30097](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30097))
- LoRA name in BlockStored for KV-cache reconstruction ( [#27577](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27577))
- Request ID collision prevention ( [#27987](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27987))
- Dense model DP without overhead ( [#30739](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30739))
- Async + spec decode penalties/bad\\_words ( [#30495](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30495))
### Hardware & Performance
\*\*CUTLASS MoE Optimizations:\*\*
- 2.9% throughput + 10.8% TTFT via fill(0) optimization ( [#31754](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31754))
- 5.3% throughput + 2.2% TTFT via problem size calculation ( [#31830](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31830))
- Fused SiLU+Mul+Quant for NVFP4 ( [#31832](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31832))
- NVFP4 stride fusion ( [#31837](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31837))
\*\*Other Performance:\*\*
- GDN attention decode speedup (Qwen3-Next) ( [#31722](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31722))
- Fused RoPE + MLA KV-cache write ( [#25774](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25774))
- Sliding window attention optimization ( [#31984](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31984))
- FlashInfer DeepGEMM swapAB SM90 ( [#29213](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29213))
- Unpermute-aware fused MoE + small-batch fallback ( [#29354](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29354))
- GDN Attention blocking copy removal ( [#31167](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31167))
- FusedMoE LoRA small rank performance ( [#32019](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32019))
- EPLB numpy optimization ( [#29499](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29499))
- FlashInfer rotary for DeepSeek ( [#30729](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30729))
- Vectorized activations ( [#29512](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29512))
- NUMA interleaved memory ( [#30800](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30800))
- Async spec decode logprobs ( [#31336](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31336))
\*\*Hardware Configs:\*\*
- SM103 support ( [#30705](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30705), [#31150](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31150))
- B300 Blackwell MoE configs ( [#30629](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30629))
- Qwen3-Next FP8 CUTLASS configs ( [#29553](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29553))
- Qwen3Moe B200 Triton configs ( [#31448](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31448))
- GLM-4.5/4.6 RTX Pro 6000 kernels ( [#31407](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31407))
- MiniMax-M2/M2.1 QKNorm ( [#31493](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31493))
- NVFP4 small batch tuning ( [#30897](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30897))
\*\*Platform:\*\*
- ROCm: AITER RMSNorm fusion ( [#26575](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26575)), MTP for AITER MLA ( [#28624](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28624)), moriio connector ( [#29304](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29304)), xgrammar upstream ( [#31327](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31327))
- XPU: FP8 streaming quant ( [#30944](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30944)), custom workers ( [#30935](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30935))
- CPU: Head sizes 80/112 ( [#31968](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31968)), async disabled by default ( [#31525](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31525)), LoRA MoE CPU pinning ( [#31317](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31317))
- TPU: tpu-inference path ( [#30808](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30808)), Sophgo docs ( [#30949](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30949))
### Large Scale Serving

### Quantization

### Security
- Prevent token leaks in crash logs ( [#30751](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30751))
- `weights\_only=True` in torch.load ( [#32045](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32045))
### Dependencies
- \*\*PyTorch 2.9.1\*\* ( [#28495](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28495))
- compressed-tensors 0.13.0 ( [#30799](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30799))
- CUDA 13 LMCache/NIXL in Docker ( [#30913](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30913))
- Configurable NVSHMEM version ( [#30732](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30732))
### Bug Fixes (User-Facing)
- Invalid UTF-8 tokens ( [#28874](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28874))
- CPU RoPE gibberish with `--enforce-eager` ( [#31643](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31643))
- Tool call streaming finish chunk ( [#31438](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31438))
- Encoder cache leak CPU scheduling stuck ( [#31857](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31857))
- Engine crash: tools + response\\_format ( [#32127](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32127))
- Voxtral transcription API ( [#31388](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31388))
- Safetensors download optimization ( [#30537](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30537))
### Deprecations
- Deprecated quantization schemes removed ( [#31688](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31688), [#31285](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31285))
- `seed\_everything` deprecated ( [#31659](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31659))
### Documentation
- vllm-metal plugin docs ( [#31174](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31174))

- CustomOp developer guide ( [#30886](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30886))

# vLLM v0.13.0 Release Notes Highlights
## Highlights
This release features \*\*442 commits from 207 contributors (61 new contributors)!\*\*
\*\*Breaking Changes\*\*: This release includes deprecation removals, PassConfig flag renames, and attention configuration changes from environment variables to CLI arguments. Please review the breaking changes section carefully before upgrading.
### Model Support
- \*\*New models\*\*: BAGEL (AR only) ( [#28439](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28439)), AudioFlamingo3 ( [#30539](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30539)), JAIS 2 ( [#30188](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30188)), latent MoE architecture support ( [#30203](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30203)).
- \*\*Tool parsers\*\*: DeepSeek-V3.2 ( [#29848](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29848)), Gigachat 3 ( [#29905](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29905)), Holo2 reasoning ( [#30048](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30048)).
- \*\*Model enhancements\*\*: Qwen3-VL embeddings support ( [#30037](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30037)), Qwen3-VL EVS (Efficient Video Sampling) ( [#29752](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29752)), DeepSeek V3.2 proper `drop\_thinking` logic ( [#30490](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30490)), DeepSeek V3.2 top-k fix ( [#27568](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27568)).
- \*\*Task expansion\*\*: Automatic TokenClassification model conversion ( [#30666](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30666)), Ultravox v0.7 transformer projector ( [#30089](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30089)).
- \*\*Quantization\*\*: BitsAndBytes for Qwen3-Omni-MoE ( [#29896](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29896)).
- \*\*Speculative decoding\*\*: Eagle/Eagle3 Transformers backend ( [#30340](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30340)), Mamba `selective\_state\_update` spec decode ( [#29488](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29488)).
### Engine Core
- \*\*Compilation\*\*: Conditional compilation via `compile\_ranges` for selective kernel compilation ( [#24252](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24252)).
- \*\*Prefix caching\*\*: xxHash high-performance hash option ( [#29163](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29163)).
- \*\*Attention\*\*: PrefixLM support for FlexAttention ( [#27938](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27938)) and TritonAttention ( [#30386](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30386)), CUDA graphs for 3D Triton attention ( [#28306](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28306)), `TRITON\_MLA` without prefix-caching ( [#29125](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29125)).
- \*\*Batch invariance\*\*: FA2 and LoRA batch-invariant support ( [#30018](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30018)).
- \*\*Pooling\*\*: Chunked prefill for ALL pooling tasks ( [#27145](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27145)), multi-vector retrieval API ( [#26686](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26686)).
- \*\*Model Runner V2\*\*: Min-p sampling ( [#30171](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30171)), NaN detection in logits ( [#30187](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30187)).
- \*\*Speculative decoding\*\*: Medusa GPU-CPU sync avoidance ( [#29723](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29723)), async spec-decode improvements ( [#29624](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29624)).
- \*\*Whisper\*\*: Major performance improvements - [V1 is now faster than V0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/24946#issuecomment-3680725754) (~3x speedup vs v0.12.0). Encoder batching ( [#29421](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29421)), `FULL\_DECODE\_ONLY` CUDA graph ( [#30072](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30072)), CPU backend support ( [#30062](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30062)).
- \*\*Performance\*\*: Fused blockwise quant RMS norm ( [#27883](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27883)), MoE LoRA loading reduction ( [#30243](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30243)), encoder cache optimization ( [#30475](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30475)), CPU KV offloading streams ( [#29013](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29013)).
### Hardware & Performance
- \*\*NVIDIA Blackwell Ultra\*\*: SM103 (GB300) support with CUDA 13 ( [#30484](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30484)).
- \*\*DeepSeek optimizations\*\*(benchmarked on DeepSeek-V3.1):
- DeepEP High-Throughput CUDA graph enabled by default: \*\*5.3% throughput, 4.4% TTFT improvement\*\* ( [#29558](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29558))
- DeepGEMM fused layout kernel: \*\*4.3% throughput, 10.7% TTFT improvement\*\* ( [#29546](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29546))
- DeepGEMM experts initialization: \*\*3.9% TTFT improvement\*\* ( [#30494](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30494))
- `group\_topk` kernel: \*\*1.9% throughput, 2.1% TPOT improvement\*\* ( [#30159](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30159))
- Sparse prefill kernel for FP8 KV-cache in DeepSeek-V3.2 ( [#27532](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27532))
- MLA FP8 optimization with ReduceScatterSum ( [#29795](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29795)), direct k\\_nope/k\\_pe copy ( [#29710](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29710))
- \*\*CPU\*\*: Whisper support ( [#30062](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30062)), Arm Optimized Routines vectorized exp ( [#30068](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30068)), x86 CPU wheel pipeline ( [#28848](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28848)).
- \*\*AMD ROCm\*\*: Aiter quantization kernels ( [#25552](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25552)), torch.compile layernorm/silu + FP8 quant ( [#25693](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25693)), Triton ScaledMM fallback ( [#26668](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26668)), MXFP4 w4a4 inference ( [#29775](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29775)).
- \*\*Intel XPU\*\*: wNa16 compressed tensors ( [#29484](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29484)).
- \*\*Build\*\*: CUDA 13 aarch64 wheels ( [#30341](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30341)), Docker kernel build stage ( [#29452](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29452)), Ascend NPU Docker ( [#30015](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30015)).
### Large Scale Serving & Disaggregated Prefill/Decode
- \*\*KV connectors\*\*: Mooncake Transfer Engine ( [#24718](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24718)), cache reset via `/reset\_prefix\_cache` ( [#27170](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27170)), KV events ( [#28309](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28309)), failure recovery config ( [#26813](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26813)).
- \*\*NIXL\*\*: Compatibility checking in handshake ( [#29503](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29503)), large batch proxy support ( [#28782](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28782)).
- \*\*EPLB\*\*: NVFP4 support ( [#29804](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29804)), algorithm abstraction ( [#26471](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26471)).
- \*\*Multi-node\*\*: External launcher mode ( [#29833](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29833)).
- \*\*Hybrid allocator\*\*: Optional KV connector integration ( [#29805](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29805)).
- \*\*Performance\*\*: silu\\_mul\\_per\\_token\\_group\\_quant\\_fp8 kernel for DP/EP ( [#29470](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29470)).
### Quantization
- \*\*New\*\*: W4A8 grouped GEMM on Hopper ( [#29691](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29691)), online FP8 with streaming post-processing ( [#29196](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29196)), FP8 weight reloading for RLHF ( [#28480](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28480)).
- \*\*MoE + LoRA\*\*: AWQ Marlin ( [#30442](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30442)) and GPTQ Marlin ( [#30254](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30254)) support.
- \*\*GGUF\*\*: MoE + GGUF restored for Qwen3 MoE ( [#30116](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30116)), Qwen2 MoE ( [#30307](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30307)), HF defaults override ( [#30118](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30118)).
- \*\*Compatibility\*\*: Transformers v5 RoPE support ( [#30046](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30046)).
### API & Frontend
- \*\*Responses API\*\*: MCP type infrastructure ( [#30054](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30054)), Browser/Container MCP tools ( [#29989](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29989)), full MCP Python loop ( [#29798](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29798)), extra body parameters ( [#30532](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30532)).
- \*\*Configuration\*\*: `AttentionConfig` replaces `VLLM\_ATTENTION\_BACKEND` env var ( [#26315](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26315)).
- \*\*Chat templates\*\*: DeepSeek-V3.2 ( [#29837](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29837)), DeepSeek-V3.2 developer tools ( [#30040](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30040)).
- \*\*Anthropic API\*\*: Streaming fixes ( [#29971](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29971), [#30266](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30266)).
- \*\*Embeddings\*\*: Binary format with `encoding\_format=bytes\_only` ( [#30249](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30249)), multiple image/audio per request ( [#29988](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29988)), tokenization\\_kwargs override ( [#29794](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29794)).
- \*\*Metrics\*\*: Prefill KV compute metric excluding cached tokens ( [#30189](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30189)).
- \*\*Profiling\*\*: Layer-wise NVTX ( [#29990](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29990)), profiling CLI config ( [#29912](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29912)).
- \*\*UX\*\*: Better OOM errors ( [#28051](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28051)), ModelConfig validation ( [#30213](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30213)), distributed executor errors ( [#30140](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30140)).
### Security
- Additional protection for [CVE-2025-62164](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-mrw7-hf4f-83pf "CVE-2025-62164") ( [#30649](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30649)).
### Dependencies
- NVSHMEM 3.3.24 + CUDA 13 fix ( [#30149](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30149)).
- TPU tpu-inference 0.12.0 ( [#30221](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30221)).
### Breaking Changes & Deprecations
1. \*\*PassConfig flags renamed\*\* per RFC [#27995](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/27995) ( [#29646](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29646))
2. \*\*Attention env vars → CLI args\*\*: `VLLM\_ATTENTION\_BACKEND` replaced with `--attention-backend` ( [#26315](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26315))
3. \*\*Removed `-O.xx` flag\*\* ( [#29991](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29991))
4. \*\*Removed deprecated plugin/compilation fields\*\* ( [#30396](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30396))
5. \*\*Removed deprecated task, seed, MM settings\*\* ( [#30397](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30397))
6. \*\*Removed `embed\_input\_ids`/`embed\_multimodal` fallbacks\*\* ( [#30458](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30458))
7. \*\*Removed tokenizer setter\*\* ( [#30400](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30400))
8. \*\*Deprecations\*\*: `merge\_by\_field\_config` ( [#30035](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30035), [#30170](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30170)), `--convert reward` → `--convert embed` ( [#30463](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30463))

# vLLM v0.12.0 Release Notes Highlights
## Highlights
This release features 474 commits from 213 contributors (57 new)！
\*\*Breaking Changes\*\*: This release includes PyTorch 2.9.0 upgrade (CUDA 12.9), V0 deprecations including `xformers` backend, and scheduled removals - please review the changelog carefully.
\*\*Major Features\*\*:
- \*\*EAGLE Speculative Decoding Improvements\*\*: Multi-step CUDA graph support ( [#29559](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29559)), DP>1 support ( [#26086](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26086)), and multimodal support with Qwen3VL ( [#29594](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29594)).
- \*\*Significant Performance Optimizations\*\*: 18.1% throughput improvement from batch invariant BMM ( [#29345](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29345)), 2.2% throughput improvement from shared experts overlap ( [#28879](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28879)).
- \*\*AMD ROCm Expansion\*\*: DeepSeek v3.2 + SparseMLA support ( [#26670](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26670)), FP8 MLA decode ( [#28032](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28032)), AITER attention backend ( [#28701](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28701)).
### Model Support
- \*\*New model families\*\*: PLaMo-3 ( [#28834](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28834)), OpenCUA-7B ( [#29068](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29068)), HunyuanOCR ( [#29327](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29327)), Mistral Large 3 and Ministral 3 ( [#29757](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29757)).
- \*\*Format support\*\*: Gemma3 GGUF multimodal support ( [#27772](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27772)).
- \*\*Multimodal enhancements\*\*: Qwen3 Omni audio-in-video support ( [#27721](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27721)), Eagle3 multimodal support for Qwen3VL ( [#29594](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29594)).
- \*\*Performance\*\*: QwenVL cos/sin cache optimization ( [#28798](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28798)).
### Engine Core
- \*\*GPU Model Runner V2 (Experimental)\*\* ( [#25266](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25266)): Complete refactoring of model execution pipeline:
- No "reordering" or complex bookkeeping with persistent batch removal
- GPU-persistent block tables for better scalability with `max\_model\_len` and `num\_kv\_groups`
- Triton-native sampler: no -1 temperature hack, efficient per-request seeds, memory-efficient prompt logprobs
- Simplified DP and CUDA graph implementations
- Efficient structured outputs support
- \*\*Prefill Context Parallel (PCP) (Preparatory)\*\* ( [#28718](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28718)): Partitions the sequence dimension during prefill for improved long-sequence inference. Complements existing Decode Context Parallel (DCP). See RFC [#25749](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/25749) for details.
- \*\*RLHF Support\*\*: Pause and Resume Generation for Asynchronous RL Training ( [#28037](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28037)).
- \*\*KV Cache Enhancements\*\*: Cross-layer KV blocks support ( [#27743](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27743)), KV cache residency metrics ( [#27793](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27793)).
- \*\*Audio support\*\*: Audio embeddings support in chat completions ( [#29059](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29059)).
- \*\*Speculative Decoding\*\*:
- Multi-step Eagle with CUDA graph ( [#29559](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29559))
- EAGLE DP>1 support ( [#26086](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26086))
- EAGLE3 heads without `use\_aux\_hidden\_states` ( [#27688](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27688))
- Eagle multimodal CUDA graphs with MRoPE ( [#28896](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28896))
- Logprobs support with spec decode + async scheduling ( [#29223](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29223))
- \*\*Configuration\*\*: Flexible `inputs\_embeds\_size` separate from `hidden\_size` ( [#29741](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29741)), `--fully-sharded-loras` for fused\\_moe ( [#28761](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28761)).
### Hardware & Performance
- \*\*NVIDIA Performance\*\*:
- \*\*Batch invariant BMM optimization\*\*: 18.1% throughput improvement, 10.7% TTFT improvement on DeepSeek-V3.1 ( [#29345](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29345))
- \*\*Shared Experts Overlap with FlashInfer DeepGEMM\*\*: 2.2% throughput improvement, 3.6% TTFT improvement at batch size 32 ( [#28879](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28879))
- DeepGEMM N dim restriction reduced from 128 to 64 multiplier ( [#28687](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28687))
- DeepEP low-latency with round-robin expert placement ( [#28449](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28449))
- NVFP4 MoE CUTLASS support for SM120 ( [#29242](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29242))
- H200 Fused MoE Config improvements ( [#28992](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28992))
- \*\*AMD ROCm\*\*:
- DeepSeek v3.2 and SparseMLA support ( [#26670](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26670))
- FP8 MLA decode support ( [#28032](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28032))
- AITER sampling ops integration ( [#26084](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26084))
- AITER triton attention backend ( [#28701](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28701))
- Bitsandbytes quantization on AMD GPUs with warp size 32 ( [#27307](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27307))
- Fastsafetensors support ( [#28225](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28225))
- Sliding window support for AiterFlashAttentionBackend ( [#29234](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29234))
- Whisper v1 with Aiter Unified/Flash Attention ( [#28376](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28376))
- \*\*CPU\*\*:
- Paged attention GEMM acceleration on ARM CPUs with NEON ( [#29193](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29193))
- Parallelize over tokens in int4 MoE ( [#29600](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29600))
- CPU all reduce optimization for async\\_scheduling + DP>1 ( [#29311](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29311))
- \*\*Attention\*\*: FlashAttention ViT support, now default backend ( [#28763](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28763)).
- \*\*Long Context\*\*: Optimized `gather\_and\_maybe\_dequant\_cache` kernel for extremely long sequences ( [#28029](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28029)).
- \*\*Multi-NUMA\*\*: Enhanced NUMA functionality for systems with multiple NUMA nodes per socket ( [#25559](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25559)).
- \*\*Docker\*\*: Image size reduced by ~200MB ( [#29060](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29060)).
### Quantization
- \*\*W4A8\*\*: Marlin kernel support ( [#24722](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24722)).
- \*\*NVFP4\*\*:
- MoE CUTLASS support for SM120 ( [#29242](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29242))
- TRTLLM MoE NVFP4 kernel ( [#28892](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28892))
- CuteDSL MoE with NVFP4 DeepEP dispatch ( [#27141](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/27141))
- Non-gated activations support in modelopt path ( [#29004](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29004))
- \*\*AWQ\*\*: Compressed-tensors AWQ support for Turing GPUs ( [#29732](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29732)).
- \*\*LoRA\*\*: FusedMoE LoRA Triton kernel for MXFP4 ( [#29708](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29708)).
- \*\*Online quantization\*\*: Moved to `model.load\_weights` ( [#26327](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26327)).
### API & Frontend
- \*\*Responses API\*\*:
- Multi-turn support for non-harmony requests ( [#29175](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29175))
- Reasoning item input parsing ( [#28248](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28248))
- \*\*Tool Calling\*\*:
- Parsed tool arguments support ( [#28820](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28820))
- `parallel\_tool\_calls` param compliance ( [#26233](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26233))
- Tool filtering support in ToolServer ( [#29224](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29224))
- \*\*Whisper\*\*: `verbose\_json` and `timestamp` features for transcription/translation ( [#24209](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24209)).
- \*\*Sampling\*\*: Flat logprob control moved from env var to `SamplingParams` ( [#28914](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28914)).
- \*\*GGUF\*\*: Improved HuggingFace loading UX with `repo\_id:quant\_type` syntax ( [#29137](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29137)).
- \*\*Profiling\*\*: Iteration-level profiling for Torch and CUDA profiler ( [#28987](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28987)).
- \*\*Logs\*\*: Colorized log output ( [#29017](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29017)).
- \*\*Optimization Levels\*\*: `-O0`, `-O1`, `-O2`, `-O3` allow trading startup time for performance, more compilation flags will be added in future releases ( [#26847](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26847))
### Dependencies
- \*\*PyTorch 2.9.0\*\* with CUDA 12.9 ( [#24994](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24994)) \- \*\*Breaking change\*\* requiring environment updates.
- \*\*xgrammar\*\*: Updated to 0.1.27 ( [#28221](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28221)).
- \*\*Transformers\*\*: Updated to 4.57.3 ( [#29418](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29418)), preparation for v5 with `rope\_parameters` ( [#28542](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28542)).
- \*\*XPU\*\*: torch & IPEX 2.9 upgrade ( [#29307](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29307)).
### V0 Deprecation & Breaking Changes
\*\*Removed Parameters\*\*:
- `num\_lookahead\_slots` ( [#29000](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29000))
- `best\_of` ( [#29090](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29090))
- LoRA extra vocab ( [#28545](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28545))
\*\*Deprecated\*\*:
- `xformers` backend ( [#29262](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29262))
- `seed=None` ( [#29185](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29185))
\*\*Scheduled Removals\*\* (will be removed in future release):
- `ParallelConfig`'s direct child EPLB fields ( [#29324](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29324))
- `guided\_\*` config fields ( [#29326](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29326))
- `override\_pooler\_config` and `disable\_log\_requests` ( [#29402](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29402))
- `CompilationConfig.use\_inductor` ( [#29323](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29323))
- Deprecated metrics ( [#29330](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/29330))
\*\*Other Breaking Changes\*\*:
- PyTorch 2.9.0 upgrade requires CUDA 12.9 environment
- Mistral format auto-detection for model loading ( [#28659](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28659))

This release includes 4 bug fixes on top of `v0.11.1`:
- \[BugFix\] Ray with multiple nodes ( [#28873](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/28873))

[v0.11.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.11.1)
## Highlights
This release includes 1456 commits from 449 contributors (184 new contributors)!
Key changes include:
- \*\*PyTorch 2.9.0 + CUDA 12.9.1\*\*: Updated the default CUDA build to `torch==2.9.0+cu129`, enabling Inductor partitioning and landing multiple fixes in graph-partition rules and compile-cache integration.
- \*\*Batch-invariant `torch.compile`\*\*: Generalized batch-invariant support across attention and MoE backends, with explicit support for DeepGEMM and FlashInfer on Hopper and Blackwell GPUs.
- \*\*Robust async scheduling\*\*: Fixed several correctness and stability issues in async scheduling, especially when combined with chunked prefill, structured outputs, priority scheduling, MTP, and DeepEP / DCP. We expect `--async-scheduling` to be enabled by default in the next release.
- \*\*Stronger scheduler + KV ecosystem\*\*: Improved test coverage in CI and made scheduler behavior more robust with KV connectors, prefix caching, and multi-node deployments.
- \*\*Anthropic API Support\*\*: Added support for the `/v1/messages` endpoint, allowing users to interact with `vllm serve` using Anthropic-compatible clients.
Detailed release notes will be updated in the next few days.

[v0.11.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.11.0)
## Highlights
This release features 538 commits, 207 contributors (65 new contributors)!
- This release completes the removal of V0 engine. V0 engine code including AsyncLLMEngine, LLMEngine, MQLLMEngine, all attention backends, and related components have been removed. \*\*V1 is the only engine in the codebase now.\*\*
- This releases turns on \*\*FULL\\_AND\\_PIECEWISE as the CUDA graph mode default\*\*. This should provide better out of the box performance for most models, particularly fine-grained MoEs, while preserving compatibility with existing models supporting only PIECEWISE mode.
Note: In v0.11.0 (and v0.10.2), `--async-scheduling` will produce gibberish output in some cases such as preemption and others. This functionality is correct in v0.10.1. We are actively fixing it for the next version.
### Model Support
- New architectures: DeepSeek-V3.2-Exp ( [#25896](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25896)), Qwen3-VL series ( [#24727](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24727)), Qwen3-Next ( [#24526](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24526)), OLMo3 ( [#24534](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24534)), LongCat-Flash ( [#23991](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23991)), Dots OCR ( [#24645](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24645)), Ling2.0 ( [#24627](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24627)), CWM ( [#25611](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25611)).
- Encoders: RADIO encoder support ( [#24595](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24595)), Transformers backend support for encoder-only models ( [#25174](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25174)).
- Task expansion: BERT token classification/NER ( [#24872](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24872)), multimodal models for pooling tasks ( [#24451](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24451)).
- Data parallel for vision encoders: InternVL ( [#23909](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23909)), Qwen2-VL ( [#25445](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25445)), Qwen3-VL ( [#24955](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24955)).
- Speculative decoding: EAGLE3 for MiniCPM3 ( [#24243](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24243)) and GPT-OSS ( [#25246](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25246)).
- Features: Qwen3-VL text-only mode ( [#26000](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/26000)), EVS video token pruning ( [#22980](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22980)), Mamba2 TP+quantization ( [#24593](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24593)), MRoPE + YaRN ( [#25384](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25384)), Whisper on XPU ( [#25123](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25123)), LongCat-Flash-Chat tool calling ( [#24083](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24083)).
- Performance: GLM-4.1V 916ms TTFT reduction via fused RMSNorm ( [#24733](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24733)), GLM-4 MoE SharedFusedMoE optimization ( [#24849](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24849)), Qwen2.5-VL CUDA sync removal ( [#24741](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24741)), Qwen3-VL Triton MRoPE kernel ( [#25055](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25055)), FP8 checkpoints for Qwen3-Next ( [#25079](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25079)).
- Reasoning: SeedOSS reason parser ( [#24263](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24263)).
### Engine Core
- KV cache offloading: CPU offloading with LRU management ( [#19848](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/19848), [#20075](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20075), [#21448](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21448), [#22595](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22595), [#24251](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24251)).
- V1 features: Prompt embeddings ( [#24278](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24278)), sharded state loading ( [#25308](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25308)), FlexAttention sliding window ( [#24089](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24089)), LLM.apply\\_model ( [#18465](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18465)).
- Hybrid allocator: Pipeline parallel ( [#23974](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23974)), varying hidden sizes ( [#25101](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25101)).
- Async scheduling: Uniprocessor executor support ( [#24219](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24219)).
- Architecture: Tokenizer group removal ( [#24078](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24078)), shared memory multimodal caching ( [#20452](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20452)).
- Attention: Hybrid SSM/Attention in Triton ( [#21197](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21197)), FlashAttention 3 for ViT ( [#24347](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24347)).
- Performance: FlashInfer RoPE 2x speedup ( [#21126](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21126)), fused Q/K RoPE 11% improvement ( [#24511](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24511), [#25005](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25005)), 8x spec decode overhead reduction ( [#24986](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24986)), FlashInfer spec decode with 1.14x speedup ( [#25196](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25196)), model info caching ( [#23558](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23558)), inputs\\_embeds copy avoidance ( [#25739](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25739)).
- LoRA: Optimized weight loading ( [#25403](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25403)).
- Defaults: CUDA graph mode FULL\\_AND\\_PIECEWISE ( [#25444](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25444)), Inductor standalone compile disabled ( [#25391](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25391)).
- torch.compile: CUDA graph Inductor partition integration ( [#24281](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24281)).
### Hardware & Performance
- NVIDIA: FP8 FlashInfer MLA decode ( [#24705](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24705)), BF16 fused MoE for Hopper/Blackwell expert parallel ( [#25503](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25503)).
- DeepGEMM: Enabled by default ( [#24462](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24462)), 5.5% throughput improvement ( [#24783](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24783)).
- New architectures: RISC-V 64-bit ( [#22112](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22112)), ARM non-x86 CPU ( [#25166](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25166)), ARM 4-bit fused MoE ( [#23809](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23809)).
- AMD: ROCm 7.0 ( [#25178](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25178)), GLM-4.5 MI300X tuning ( [#25703](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25703)).
- Intel XPU: MoE DP accuracy fix ( [#25465](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25465)).
### Large Scale Serving & Performance
- Dual-Batch Overlap (DBO): Overlapping computation mechanism ( [#23693](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23693)), DeepEP high throughput + prefill ( [#24845](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24845)).
- Data Parallelism: torchrun launcher ( [#24899](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24899)), Ray placement groups ( [#25026](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25026)), Triton DP/EP kernels ( [#24588](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24588)).
- EPLB: Hunyuan V1 ( [#23078](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23078)), Mixtral ( [#22842](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22842)), static placement ( [#23745](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23745)), reduced overhead ( [#24573](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24573)).
- Disaggregated serving: KV transfer metrics ( [#22188](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22188)), NIXL MLA latent dimension ( [#25902](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25902)).
- MoE: Shared expert overlap optimization ( [#24254](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24254)), SiLU kernel for DeepSeek-R1 ( [#24054](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24054)), Enable Allgather/ReduceScatter backend for NaiveAllToAll ( [#23964](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23964)).
- Distributed: NCCL symmetric memory with 3-4% throughput improvement ( [#24532](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24532)), enabled by default for TP ( [#25070](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25070)).
### Quantization
- FP8: Per-token-group quantization ( [#24342](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24342)), hardware-accelerated instructions ( [#24757](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24757)), torch.compile KV cache ( [#22758](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22758)), paged attention update ( [#22222](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22222)).
- FP4: NVFP4 for dense models ( [#25609](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25609)), Gemma3 ( [#22771](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22771)), Llama 3.1 405B ( [#25135](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25135)).
- W4A8: Faster preprocessing ( [#23972](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23972)).
- Compressed tensors: Blocked FP8 for MoE ( [#25219](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25219)).
### API & Frontend
- OpenAI: Prompt logprobs for all tokens ( [#24956](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24956)), logprobs=-1 for full vocab ( [#25031](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25031)), reasoning streaming events ( [#24938](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24938)), Responses API MCP tools ( [#24628](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24628), [#24985](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24985)), health 503 on dead engine ( [#24897](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24897)).
- Multimodal: Media UUID caching ( [#23950](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23950)), image path format ( [#25081](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25081)).
- Tool calling: XML parser for Qwen3-Coder ( [#25028](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25028)), Hermes-style tokens ( [#25281](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25281)).
- CLI: --enable-logging ( [#25610](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25610)), improved --help ( [#24903](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24903)).
- Config: Speculative model engine args ( [#25250](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25250)), env validation ( [#24761](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24761)), NVTX profiling ( [#25501](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25501)), guided decoding backward compatibility ( [#25615](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25615), [#25422](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25422)).
- Metrics: V1 TPOT histogram ( [#24015](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24015)), hidden deprecated gpu\\_ metrics ( [#24245](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24245)), KV cache GiB units ( [#25204](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25204), [#25479](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25479)).
- UX: Removed misleading quantization warning ( [#25012](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25012)).
### Security
- [GHSA-wr9h-g72x-mwhm](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/security/advisories/GHSA-wr9h-g72x-mwhm "GHSA-wr9h-g72x-mwhm")
### Dependencies
- PyTorch 2.8 for CPU ( [#25652](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25652)), FlashInfer 0.3.1 ( [#24470](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24470)), CUDA 13 ( [#24599](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24599)), ROCm 7.0 ( [#25178](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25178)).
- \*\*Build requirements\*\*: C++17 now enforced globally ( [#24823](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24823)).
- \*\*TPU\*\*: Deprecated `xm.mark\_step` in favor of `torch\_xla.sync` ( [#25254](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25254)).
### V0 Deprecation
- Engines: AsyncLLMEngine ( [#25025](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25025)), LLMEngine ( [#25033](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25033)), MQLLMEngine ( [#25019](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25019)), core ( [#25321](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25321)), model runner ( [#25328](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25328)), MP executor ( [#25329](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25329)).
- Components: Attention backends ( [#25351](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25351)), encoder-decoder ( [#24907](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24907)), output processor ( [#25320](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25320)), sampling metadata ( [#25345](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25345)), Sequence/Sampler ( [#25332](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25332)).
- Interfaces: LoRA ( [#25686](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25686)), async output processor ( [#25334](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25334)), MultiModalPlaceholderMap ( [#25366](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25366)), seq group methods ( [#25330](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25330)), placeholder attention ( [#25510](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25510)), input embeddings ( [#25242](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25242)), multimodal registry ( [#25362](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25362)), max\\_seq\\_len\\_to\\_capture ( [#25543](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25543)), attention classes ( [#25541](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25541)), hybrid models ( [#25400](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25400)), backend suffixes ( [#25489](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25489)), compilation fallbacks ( [#25675](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25675)), default args ( [#25409](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/25409)).
## What's Changed
- \[Qwen3-Next\] MoE configs for H20 TP=1,2,4,8 by [@jeejeelee](https://github.com/jeejeelee) in [#24707](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24707)
- \[DOCs\] Update ROCm installation docs section by [@gshtras](https://github.com/gshtras) in [#24691](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24691)
- Enable conversion of multimodal models to pooling tasks by [@maxdebayser](https://github.com/maxdebayser) in [#24451](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24451)
- Fix implementation divergence for BLOOM models between vLLM and HuggingFace when using prompt embeds by [@qthequartermasterman](https://github.com/qthequartermasterman) in [#24686](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24686)
- \[Bugfix\] Fix MRoPE dispatch on CPU by [@bigPYJ1151](https://github.com/bigPYJ1151) in [#24712](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24712)
- \[BugFix\] Fix Qwen3-Next PP by [@njhill](https://github.com/njhill) in [#24709](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24709)
- \[CI\] Fix flaky test v1/worker/test\\_gpu\\_model\\_runner.py::test\\_kv\\_cache\\_stride\\_order by [@heheda12345](https://github.com/heheda12345) in [#24640](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24640)
- \[CI\] Add ci\\_envs for convenient local testing by [@noooop](https://github.com/noooop) in [#24630](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24630)
- \[CI/Build\] Skip prompt embeddings tests on V1-only CPU backend by [@bigPYJ1151](https://github.com/bigPYJ1151) in [#24721](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24721)
- \[Misc\]\[gpt-oss\] Add gpt-oss label to PRs that mention harmony or related to builtin tool call by [@heheda12345](https://github.com/heheda12345) in [#24717](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24717)
- \[Bugfix\] Fix BNB name match by [@jeejeelee](https://github.com/jeejeelee) in [#24735](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24735)
- \[Kernel\] \[CPU\] refactor `cpu\_attn.py:\_run\_sdpa\_forward` for better memory access by [@ignaciosica](https://github.com/ignaciosica) in [#24701](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24701)
- \[sleep mode\] save memory for on-the-fly quantization by [@youkaichao](https://github.com/youkaichao) in [#24731](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24731)
- \[Multi Modal\] Add FA3 in VIT by [@wwl2755](https://github.com/wwl2755) in [#24347](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24347)
- \[Multimodal\] Remove legacy multimodal fields in favor of MultiModalFeatureSpec by [@sfeng33](https://github.com/sfeng33) in [#24548](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24548)
- \[Doc\]: fix typos in various files by [@didier-durand](https://github.com/didier-durand) in [#24726](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24726)
- \[Docs\] Fix warnings in mkdocs build (continued) by [@Zerohertz](https://github.com/Zerohertz) in [#24740](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24740)
- \[Bugfix\] Fix MRoPE dispatch on XPU by [@yma11](https://github.com/yma11) in [#24724](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24724)
- \[Qwen3-Next\] MoE configs for H100 TP=1,2 and TP2/EP by [@elvircrn](https://github.com/elvircrn) in [#24739](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24739)
- \[Core\] Shared memory based object store for Multimodal data caching and IPC by [@dongluw](https://github.com/dongluw) in [#20452](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20452)
- \[Bugfix\]\[Frontend\] Fix `--enable-log-outputs` does not match the documentation by [@kebe7jun](https://github.com/kebe7jun) in [#24626](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24626)
- \[Models\] Optimise and simplify `\_validate\_and\_reshape\_mm\_tensor` by [@lgeiger](https://github.com/lgeiger) in [#24742](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24742)
- \[Models\] Prevent CUDA sync in Qwen2.5-VL by [@lgeiger](https://github.com/lgeiger) in [#24741](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24741)
- \[Model\] Switch to Fused RMSNorm in GLM-4.1V model by [@SamitHuang](https://github.com/SamitHuang) in [#24733](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24733)
- \[UX\] Remove AsyncLLM torch profiler disabled log by [@mgoin](https://github.com/mgoin) in [#24609](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24609)
- \[CI\] Speed up model unit tests in CI by [@afeldman-nm](https://github.com/afeldman-nm) in [#24253](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24253)
- \[Bugfix\] Fix incompatibility between [#20452](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20452) and [#24548](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24548) by [@DarkLight1337](https://github.com/DarkLight1337) in [#24754](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24754)
- \[CI\] Trigger BC Linter when labels are added/removed by [@zhewenl](https://github.com/zhewenl) in [#24767](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24767)
- \[Benchmark\] Allow arbitrary headers to be passed to benchmarked endpoints by [@smarterclayton](https://github.com/smarterclayton) in [#23937](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23937)
- \[Compilation Bug\] Fix Inductor Graph Output with Shape Issue by...

## Highlights
This release contains 740 commits from 266 contributors (97 new)!
\*\*Breaking Changes\*\*: This release includes PyTorch 2.8.0 upgrade, V0 deprecations, and API changes - please review the changelog carefully.
\*\*aarch64 support\*\*: This release features native support for aarch64 allowing usage of vLLM on GB200 platform. The docker image `vllm/vllm-openai` should already be multiplatform. To install the wheels, you can download the wheels from this release artifact or install via
```
uv pip install vllm==0.10.2 --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/0.10.2/ --torch-backend=auto
```
### Model Support
- \*\*New model families and enhancements\*\*: Apertus ( [#23068](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23068)), LFM2 ( [#22845](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22845)), MiDashengLM ( [#23652](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23652)), Motif-1-Tiny ( [#23414](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23414)), Seed-Oss ( [#23241](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23241)), Google EmbeddingGemma-300m ( [#24318](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24318)), GTE sequence classification ( [#23524](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23524)), Donut OCR model ( [#23229](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23229)), KeyeVL-1.5-8B ( [#23838](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23838)), R-4B vision model ( [#23246](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23246)), Ernie4.5 VL ( [#22514](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22514)), MiniCPM-V 4.5 ( [#23586](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23586)), Ovis2.5 ( [#23084](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23084)), Qwen3-Next with hybrid attention ( [#24526](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24526)), InternVL3.5 with video support ( [#23658](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23658)), Qwen2Audio embeddings ( [#23625](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23625)), NemotronH Nano VLM ( [#23644](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23644)), BLOOM V1 engine support ( [#23488](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23488)), and Whisper encoder-decoder for V1 ( [#21088](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21088)).
- \*\*Pipeline parallelism expansion\*\*: Added PP support for Hunyuan ( [#24212](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24212)), Ovis2.5 ( [#23405](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23405)), GPT-OSS ( [#23680](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23680)), and Kimi-VL-A3B-Thinking-2506 ( [#23114](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23114)).
- \*\*Data parallelism for vision models\*\*: Enabled DP for ViT across Qwen2.5VL ( [#22742](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22742)), MiniCPM-V ( [#23948](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23948), [#23327](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23327)), Kimi-VL ( [#23817](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23817)), and GLM-4.5V ( [#23168](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23168)).
- \*\*LoRA ecosystem expansion\*\*: Added LoRA support to Voxtral ( [#24517](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24517)), Qwen-2.5-Omni ( [#24231](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24231)), and DeepSeek models V2/V3/R1-0528 ( [#23971](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23971)), with significantly faster LoRA startup performance ( [#23777](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23777)).
- \*\*Classification and pooling enhancements\*\*: Multi-label classification support ( [#23173](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23173)), logit bias and sigmoid normalization ( [#24031](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24031)), and FP32 precision heads for pooling models ( [#23810](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23810)).
- \*\*Performance optimizations\*\*: Removed unnecessary CUDA sync from GLM-4.1V ( [#24332](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24332)) and Qwen2VL ( [#24334](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24334)) preprocessing, eliminated redundant all-reduce in Qwen3 MoE ( [#23169](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23169)), optimized InternVL CPU threading ( [#24519](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24519)), and GLM4.5-V video frame decoding ( [#24161](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24161)).
### Engine Core
- \*\*V1 engine maturation\*\*: Extended V1 support to compute capability < 8.0 ( [#23614](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23614), [#24022](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24022)), added cross-attention KV cache for encoder-decoder models ( [#23664](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23664)), request-level logits processor integration ( [#23656](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23656)), and KV events from connectors ( [#19737](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/19737)).
- \*\*Backend expansion\*\*: Terratorch backend integration ( [#23513](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23513)) enabling non-language model tasks like semantic segmentation and geospatial applications with `--model-impl terratorch` support.
- \*\*Hybrid and Mamba model improvements\*\*: Enabled full CUDA graphs by default for hybrid models ( [#22594](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22594)), disabled prefix caching for hybrid/Mamba models ( [#23716](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23716)), added FP32 SSM kernel support ( [#23506](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23506)), full CUDA graph support for Mamba1 ( [#23035](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23035)), and V1 as default for Mamba models ( [#23650](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23650)).
- \*\*Performance core improvements\*\*: `--safetensors-load-strategy` for NFS based file loading acceleration ( [#24469](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24469)), critical CUDA graph capture throughput fix ( [#24128](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24128)), scheduler optimization for single completions ( [#21917](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21917)), multi-threaded model weight loading ( [#23928](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23928)), and tensor core usage enforcement for FlashInfer decode ( [#23214](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23214)).
- \*\*Multimodal enhancements\*\*: Multimodal cache tracking with mm\\_hash ( [#22711](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22711)), UUID-based multimodal identifiers ( [#23394](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23394)), improved V1 video embedding estimation ( [#24312](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24312)), and simplified multimodal UUID handling ( [#24271](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24271)).
- \*\*Sampling and structured outputs\*\*: Support for all prompt logprobs ( [#23868](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23868)), final logprobs ( [#22387](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22387)), grammar bitmask optimization ( [#23361](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23361)), and user-configurable KV cache memory size ( [#21489](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21489)).
- \*\*Distributed\*\*: Support Decode Context Parallel (DCP) for MLA ( [#23734](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23734))
### Hardware & Performance
- \*\*NVIDIA Blackwell/SM100 generation\*\*: FP8 MLA support with CUTLASS backend ( [#23289](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23289)), DeepGEMM Linear with 1.5% E2E throughput improvement ( [#23351](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23351)), Hopper DeepGEMM E8M0 for DeepSeekV3.1 ( [#23666](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23666)), SM100 FlashInfer CUTLASS MoE FP8 backend ( [#22357](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22357)), MXFP4 fused CUTLASS MoE ( [#23696](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23696)), default MXFP4 MoE on Blackwell ( [#23008](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23008)), and GPT-OSS DP/EP support with 52,003 tokens/s throughput ( [#23608](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23608)).
- \*\*Breaking change\*\*: FlashMLA disabled on Blackwell GPUs due to compatibility issues ( [#24521](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24521)).
- \*\*Kernel and attention optimizations\*\*: FlashAttention MLA with CUDA graph support ( [#14258](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/14258), [#23958](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23958)), V1 cross-attention support ( [#23297](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23297)), FP8 support for FlashMLA ( [#22668](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22668)), fused grouped TopK for MoE ( [#23274](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23274)), Flash Linear Attention kernels ( [#24518](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24518)), and W4A8 support on Hopper ( [#23198](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23198)).
- \*\*Performance improvements\*\*: 13.7x speedup for token conversion ( [#20413](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20413)), TTIT/TTFT improvements for disaggregated serving ( [#22760](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22760)), symmetric memory all-reduce by default ( [#24111](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24111)), FlashInfer warmup during startup ( [#23439](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23439)), V1 model execution overlap ( [#23569](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23569)), and various Triton configuration tuning ( [#23748](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23748), [#23939](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23939)).
- \*\*Platform expansion\*\*: Apple Silicon bfloat16 support for M2+ ( [#24129](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24129)), IBM Z V1 engine support ( [#22725](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22725)), Intel XPU torch.compile ( [#22609](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22609)), XPU MoE data parallelism ( [#22887](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22887)), XPU Triton attention ( [#24149](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24149)), XPU FP8 quantization ( [#23148](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23148)), and ROCm pipeline parallelism with Ray ( [#24275](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24275)).
- \*\*Model-specific optimizations\*\*: Hardware-tuned MoE configurations for Qwen3-Next on B200/H200/H100 ( [#24698](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24698), [#24688](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24688), [#24699](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24699), [#24695](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24695)), GLM-4.5-Air-FP8 B200 configs ( [#23695](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23695)), Kimi K2 optimization ( [#24597](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24597)), and QWEN3 Coder/Thinking configs ( [#24266](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24266), [#24330](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24330)).
### Quantization
- \*\*New quantization capabilities\*\*: Per-layer quantization routing ( [#23556](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23556)), GGUF quantization with layer skipping ( [#23188](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23188)), NFP4+FP8 MoE support ( [#22674](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22674)), W4A8 channel scales ( [#23570](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23570)), and AMD CDNA2/CDNA3 FP4 support ( [#22527](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22527)).
- \*\*Advanced quantization infrastructure\*\*: Compressed tensors transforms for linear operations ( [#22486](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22486)) enabling techniques like SpinQuantR1R2R4 and QuIP quantization methods.
- \*\*FlashInfer quantization integration\*\*: FP8 KV cache for TRTLLM prefill attention ( [#24197](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24197)), FP8-qkv attention kernels ( [#23647](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23647)), and FP8 per-tensor GEMMs ( [#22895](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22895)).
- \*\*Platform-specific quantization\*\*: ROCm TorchAO quantization enablement ( [#24400](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24400)) and TorchAO module swap configuration ( [#21982](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21982)).
- \*\*Performance optimizations\*\*: MXFP4 MoE loading cache optimization ( [#24154](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24154)) and compressed tensors version updates ( [#23202](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23202)).
- \*\*Breaking change\*\*: Removed original Marlin quantization format ( [#23204](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23204)).
### API & Frontend
- \*\*OpenAI API enhancements\*\*: Gemma3n audio transcription/translation endpoints ( [#23735](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23735)), transcription response usage statistics ( [#23576](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23576)), and return\\_token\\_ids parameter ( [#22587](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22587)).
- \*\*Response API improvements\*\*: Streaming support for non-harmony responses ( [#23741](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23741)), non-streaming logprobs ( [#23319](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23319)), MCP tool background mode ( [#23494](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23494)), MCP streaming+background support ( [#23927](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23927)), and tool output token reporting ( [#24285](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24285)).
- \*\*Frontend optimizations\*\*: Error stack traces with --log-error-stack ( [#22960](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22960)), collective RPC endpoint ( [#23075](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23075)), beam search concurrency optimization ( [#23599](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23599)), unnecessary detokenization skipping ( [#24236](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24236)), and custom media UUIDs ( [#23449](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23449)).
- \*\*Configuration enhancements\*\*: Formalized --mm-encoder-tp-mode flag ( [#23190](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23190)), VLLM\\_DISABLE\\_PAD\\_FOR\\_CUDAGRAPH environment variable ( [#23595](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23595)), EPLB configuration parameter ( [#20562](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20562)), embedding endpoint chat request support ( [#23931](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23931)), and LM Format Enforcer V1 integration ( [#22564](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22564)).
### Dependencies
- \*\*Major updates\*\*: PyTorch 2.8.0 upgrade ( [#20358](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20358)) \- breaking change requiring environment updates, FlashInfer v0.3.0 upgrade ( [#24086](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24086)), and FlashInfer 0.2.14.post1 maintenance update ( [#23537](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23537)).
- \*\*Supporting updates\*\*: XGrammar 0.1.23 ( [#22988](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22988)), TPU core dump fix with tpu\\_info 0.4.0 ( [#23135](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23135)), and compressed tensors version bump ( [#23202](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23202)).
- \*\*Deployment improvements\*\*: FlashInfer cubin directory environment variable ( [#22675](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22675)) for offline environments and pre-cached CUDA binaries.
### V0 Deprecation
- \*\*Backend removals\*\*: V0 Neuron backend deprecation ( [#21159](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21159)), V0 pooling model support removal ( [#23434](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23434)), V0 FlashInfer attention backend removal ( [#22776](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22776)), and V0 test cleanup ( [#23418](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23418), [#23862](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23862)).
- \*\*API breaking changes\*\*: prompt\\_token\\_ids fallback removal from LLM.generate and LLM.embed ( [#18800](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18800)), LoRA extra vocab size deprecation warning ( [#23635](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23635)), LoRA bias parameter deprecation ( [#24339](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24339)), and metrics naming change from TPOT to ITL ( [#24110](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/24110)).
### Breaking Changes
1. \*\*PyTorch 2.8.0 upgrade\*\* \- Environment dependency change requiring updated CUDA versions
2. \*\*FlashMLA Blackwell restriction\*\* \- FlashMLA disabled on Blackwell GPUs due to compatibility issues
3. \*\*V0 feature removals\*\* \- Neuron backend, pooling models, FlashInfer attention backend
4. \*\*Quantizations\*\* \- Removed quantized Mixtral hack implementation, and original Marlin format.
5. \*\*Metrics renaming\*\* \- TPOT deprecated in favor of ITL

- \[Misc\] Minor code cleanup for \\_get\\_prompt\\_logprobs\\_dict by [@WoosukKwon](https://github.com/WoosukKwon) in [#23064](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23064)
- \[Misc\] enhance static type hint by [@andyxning](https://github.com/andyxning) in [#23059](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23059)
- \[Bugfix\] fix Qwen2.5-Omni processor output mapping by [@DoubleVII](https://github.com/DoubleVII) in [#23058](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23058)
- \[Bugfix\]\[CI\] Machete kernels: deterministic ordering for more cache hits by [@andylolu2](https://github.com/andylolu2) in [#23055](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23055)
- \[Misc\] refactor function name by [@andyxning](https://github.com/andyxning) in [#23029](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23029)
- \[Misc\] Fix backward compatibility from [#23030](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23030) by [@ywang96](https://github.com/ywang96) in [#23070](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23070)
- \[XPU\] Fix compile size for xpu by [@jikunshang](https://github.com/jikunshang) in [#23069](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23069)
- \[XPU\]\[CI\]add xpu env vars in CI scripts by [@jikunshang](https://github.com/jikunshang) in [#22946](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22946)
- \[Refactor\] Define MultiModalKwargsItems separate from MultiModalKwargs by [@DarkLight1337](https://github.com/DarkLight1337) in [#23053](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23053)
- \[Bugfix\] fix IntermediateTensors equal method by [@andyxning](https://github.com/andyxning) in [https://github.com/vllm-project/](https://github.com/vllm-project/)...

This is a critical bugfix and security release:
- Fix CUTLASS MLA Full CUDAGraph ( [#23200](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23200))
- Limit HTTP header count and size ( [#23267](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23267)): [GHSA-rxc4-3w6r-4v47](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/security/advisories/GHSA-rxc4-3w6r-4v47 "GHSA-rxc4-3w6r-4v47")
- Do not use eval() to convert unknown types ( [#23266](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/23266)): [GHSA-79j6-g2m3-jgfw](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/security/advisories/GHSA-79j6-g2m3-jgfw "GHSA-79j6-g2m3-jgfw")
