[v0.10.1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.10.1)
## Highlights
v0.10.1 release includes 727 commits, 245 committers (105 new contributors).
\*\*NOTE: This release deprecates V0 FA3 support and as a result FP8 kv-cache in V0 may have issues\*\*
### Model Support
- \*\*New model families\*\*: GPT-OSS with comprehensive tool calling and streaming support ( [#22327](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22327), [#22330](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22330), [#22332](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22332), [#22335](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22335), [#22339](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22339), [#22340](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22340), [#22342](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22342)), Command-A-Vision ( [#22660](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22660)), mBART ( [#22883](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22883)), and SmolLM3 using Transformers backend ( [#22665](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22665)).
- \*\*Vision-language models\*\*: Official Eagle multimodal support with Llama4 backend ( [#20788](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20788)), Step3 vision-language models ( [#21998](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21998)), Gemma3n multimodal ( [#20495](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20495)), MiniCPM-V 4.0 ( [#22166](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22166)), HyperCLOVAX-SEED-Vision-Instruct-3B ( [#20931](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20931)), Emu3 with Transformers backend ( [#21319](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21319)), Intern-S1 ( [#21628](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21628)), and Prithvi in online serving mode ( [#21518](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21518)).
- \*\*Enhanced existing models\*\*: NemotronH support ( [#22349](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22349)), Ernie 4.5 Base 0.3B model name change ( [#21735](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21735)), GLM-4.5 series improvements ( [#22215](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22215)), Granite models with fused MoE configurations ( [#21332](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21332)) and quantized checkpoint loading ( [#22925](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22925)), Ultravox support for Llama 4 and Gemma 3 backends ( [#17818](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/17818)), Mamba1 and Jamba model support in V1 (without CUDA graphs) ( [#21249](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21249))
- \*\*Advanced model capabilities\*\*: Qwen3 EPLB ( [#20815](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20815)) and dual-chunk attention support ( [#21924](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21924)), Qwen native Eagle3 target support ( [#22333](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22333)).
- \*\*Architecture expansions\*\*: Encoder-only models without KV-cache enabling BERT-style architectures ( [#21270](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21270)), expanded tensor parallelism support in Transformers backend ( [#22651](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22651)), tensor parallelism for Deepseek\\_vl2 vision transformer ( [#21494](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21494)), and tensor/pipeline parallelism with Mamba2 kernel for PLaMo2 ( [#19674](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/19674)).
- \*\*V1 engine compatibility\*\*: Extended support for additional pooling models ( [#21747](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21747)) and Step3VisionEncoder distributed processing option ( [#22697](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22697)).
### Engine Core
- \*\*CUDA graph performance\*\*: Full CUDA graph support with separate attention routines, adding FA2 and FlashInfer compatibility ( [#20059](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20059)), plus 6% end-to-end throughput improvement from Cutlass MLA ( [#22763](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22763)).
- \*\*Attention system advances\*\*: Multiple attention metadata builders per KV cache specification ( [#21588](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21588)), tree attention backend for v1 engine (experimental) ( [#20401](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20401)), FlexAttention encoder-only support ( [#22273](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22273)), upgraded FlashAttention 3 with attention sink support ( [#22313](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22313)), and multiple attention groups for KV sharing patterns ( [#22672](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22672)).
- \*\*Speculative decoding optimizations\*\*: N-gram speculative decoding with single KMP token proposal algorithm ( [#22437](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22437)), explicit EAGLE3 interface for enhanced compatibility ( [#22642](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22642)).
- \*\*Default behavior improvements\*\*: Pooling models now default to chunked prefill and prefix caching ( [#20930](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20930)), disabled chunked local attention by default for Llama4 for better performance ( [#21761](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21761)).
- \*\*Extensibility and configuration\*\*: Model loader plugin system ( [#21067](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21067)), custom operations support for FusedMoe ( [#22509](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22509)), rate limiting with bucket algorithm for proxy server ( [#22643](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22643)), torch.compile support for bailing MoE ( [#21664](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21664)).
- \*\*Performance optimizations\*\*: Improved startup time by disabling C++ compilation of symbolic shapes ( [#20836](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20836)), enhanced headless models for pooling in Transformers backend ( [#21767](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21767)).
### Hardware & Performance
- \*\*NVIDIA Blackwell (SM100) optimizations\*\*: CutlassMLA as default backend ( [#21626](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21626)), FlashInfer MoE per-tensor scale FP8 backend ( [#21458](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21458)), SM90 CUTLASS FP8 GEMM with kernel tuning and swap AB support ( [#20396](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20396)).
- \*\*NVIDIA RTX 5090/RTX PRO 6000 (SM120) support\*\*: Block FP8 quantization ( [#22131](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22131)) and CUTLASS NVFP4 4-bit weights/activations support ( [#21309](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21309)).
- \*\*AMD ROCm platform enhancements\*\*: Flash Attention backend for Qwen-VL models ( [#22069](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22069)), AITER HIP block quantization kernels ( [#21242](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21242)), reduced device-to-host transfers ( [#22683](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22683)), and optimized kernel performance for small batch sizes 1-4 ( [#21350](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21350)).
- \*\*Attention and compute optimizations\*\*: FlashAttention 3 attention sinks performance boost ( [#22478](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22478)), Triton-based multi-dimensional RoPE replacing PyTorch implementation ( [#22375](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22375)), async tensor parallelism for scaled matrix multiplication ( [#20155](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20155)), optimized FlashInfer metadata building ( [#21137](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21137)).
- \*\*Memory and throughput improvements\*\*: Mamba2 reduced device-to-device copy overhead ( [#21075](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21075)), fused Triton kernels for RMSNorm ( [#20839](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20839), [#22184](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22184)), improved multimodal hasher performance for repeated image prompts ( [#22825](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22825)), multithreaded async multimodal loading ( [#22710](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22710)).
- \*\*Parallelization and MoE optimizations\*\*: Guided decoding throughput improvements ( [#21862](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21862)), balanced expert sharding for MoE models ( [#21497](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21497)), expanded fused kernel support for topk softmax ( [#22211](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22211)), fused MoE for nomic-embed-text-v2-moe ( [#18321](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18321)).
- \*\*Hardware compatibility and kernels\*\*: ARM CPU build fixes for systems without BF16 support ( [#21848](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21848)), Machete memory-bound performance improvements ( [#21556](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21556)), FlashInfer TRT-LLM prefill attention kernel support ( [#22095](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22095)), optimized `reshape\_and\_cache\_flash` CUDA kernel ( [#22036](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22036)), CPU transfer support in NixlConnector ( [#18293](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18293)).
- \*\*Specialized CUDA kernels\*\*: GPT-OSS activation functions ( [#22538](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22538)), RLHF weight loading acceleration ( [#21164](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21164)).
### Quantization
- \*\*Advanced quantization techniques\*\*: MXFP4 and bias support for Marlin kernel ( [#22428](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22428)), NVFP4 GEMM FlashInfer backends ( [#22346](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22346)), compressed-tensors mixed-precision model loading ( [#22468](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22468)), FlashInfer MoE support for NVFP4 ( [#21639](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21639)).
- \*\*Hardware-optimized quantization\*\*: Dynamic 4-bit quantization with Kleidiai kernels for CPU inference ( [#17112](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/17112)), TensorRT-LLM FP4 quantization optimized for MoE low-latency inference ( [#21331](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21331)).
- \*\*Expanded model quantization support\*\*: BitsAndBytes quantization for InternS1 ( [#21953](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21953)) and additional MoE models ( [#21370](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21370), [#21548](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21548)), Gemma3n quantization compatibility ( [#21974](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21974)), calibration-free RTN quantization for MoE models ( [#20766](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20766)), ModelOpt Qwen3 NVFP4 support ( [#20101](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20101)).
- \*\*Performance and compatibility improvements\*\*: CUDA kernel optimization for Int8 per-token group quantization ( [#21476](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21476)), non-contiguous tensor support in FP8 quantization ( [#21961](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21961)), automatic detection of ModelOpt quantization formats ( [#22073](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22073)).
- \*\*Breaking change\*\*: Removed AQLM quantization support ( [#22943](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22943)) \- users should migrate to alternative quantization methods.
### API & Frontend
- \*\*OpenAI API compatibility\*\*: Unix domain socket support for local communication ( [#18097](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18097)), improved error response format matching upstream specification ( [#22099](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22099)), aligned tool\\_choice="required" behavior with OpenAI when tools list is empty ( [#21052](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21052)).
- \*\*New API capabilities\*\*: Dedicated LLM.reward interface for reward models ( [#21720](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21720)), chunked processing for long inputs in embedding models ( [#22280](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22280)), AsyncLLM proper response handling for aborted requests ( [#22283](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22283)).
- \*\*Configuration and environment\*\*: Multiple API keys support for enhanced authentication ( [#18548](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18548)), custom vLLM tuned configuration paths ( [#22791](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22791)), environment variable control for logging statistics ( [#22905](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22905)), multimodal cache size ( [#22441](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22441)), and DeepGEMM E8M0 scaling behavior ( [#21968](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21968)).
- \*\*CLI and tooling improvements\*\*: V1 API support for run-batch command ( [#21541](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21541)), custom process naming for better monitoring ( [#21445](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21445)), improved help display showing available choices ( [#21760](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21760)), optional memory profiling skip for multimodal models ( [#22950](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22950)), enhanced logging of non-default arguments ( [#21680](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21680)).
- \*\*Tool and parser support\*\*: HermesToolParser for models without special tokens ( [#16890](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/16890)), multi-turn conversation benchmarking tool ( [#20267](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20267)).
- \*\*Distributed serving enhancements\*\*: Enhanced hybrid distributed serving with multiple API servers in load balancing mode ( [#21510](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21510)), request\\_id support for external load balancers ( [#21009](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21009)).
- \*\*User experience enhancements\*\*: Improved error messaging for multimodal items ( [#22114](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22114)), per-request pooling control via PoolingParams ( [#20538](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20538)).
### Dependencies
- \*\*FlashInfer updates\*\*: Updated to v0.2.8 for improved performance ( [#21385](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21385)), moved to optional dependency install with `pip install vllm[flashinfer]` for flexible installation ( [#21959](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21959)).
- \*\*Mamba SSM restructuring\*\*: Updated to version 2.2.5 ( [#21421](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21421)), removed from core requirements to reduce installation complexity ( [#22541](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22541)).
- \*\*Docker and deployment\*\*: Docker-aware precompiled wheel support for easier containerized deployment ( [#21127](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21127), [#22106](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22106)).
- \*\*Python package updates\*\*: OpenAI Python dependency updated to latest version for API compatibility ( [#22316](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22316)).
- \*\*Dependency optimizations\*\*: Removed xformers requirement for Mistral-format Pixtral and Mistral3 models ( [#21154](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21154)), deprecation warnings added for old DeepGEMM version ( [#22194](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/22194)).
### V0 Deprecation
\*\*Important\*\*: As part of the ongoing V0 engine cleanup, several breaking changes have been introduced:
- \*\*CLI flag updates\*\*: Replaced `--task` with `--runner` and `--convert` options ( [#21470](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21470)), deprecated `--disable-log-requests` in favor of `--enable-log-requests` for clearer semantics ( [#21739](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21739)), renamed `--expand-tools-even-if-tool-choice-none` to `--exclude-tools-when-tool-choice-none` for consistency ( [#20544](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/20544)).
- \*\*API cleanup\*\*: Removed previously deprecated arguments and methods as part of ongoing V0 engine codebase cleanup ( [#21907](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21907)).
## What's Changed
- Deduplicate Transformers backend code using inheritance by [@hmellor](https://github.com/hmellor) in [#21461](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21461)
- \[Bugfix\]\[ROCm\] Fix for warp\\_size uses on host by [@gshtras](https://github.com/gshtras) in [#21205](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21205)
- \[TPU\]\[Bugfix\] fix moe layer by [@yaochengji](https://github.com/yaochengji) in [#21340](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21340)
- \[v1\]\[Core\] Clean up usages of `SpecializedManager` by [@zhouwfang](https://github.com/zhouwfang) in [#21407](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21407)
- \[Misc\] Fix duplicate FusedMoEConfig debug messages by [@njhill](https://github.com/njhill) in [#21455](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21455)
- \[Core\] Support model loader plugins by [@22quinn](https://github.com/22quinn) in [#21067](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21067)
- remove GLM-4 quantization wrong Code by [@zRzRzRzRzRzRzR](https://github.com/zRzRzRzRzRzRzR) in [#21435](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/21435)
- Replace `--expand-tools-even-if-tool-choice-none` with `--exclude-tools-when-tool-choice-none` ...
